<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-48968606" data-id="48968606" data-tags="Show HN|Hardware|Open Source" data-score="2881" data-comments="343" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968606" data-time="1784472076">
<a class="thumb" href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968606" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=news.ycombinator.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>news.ycombinator.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968606" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I replaced a $120k bowling center system with $1,600 in ESP32s</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用 1,600 美元的 ESP32 替代 12 万美元的保龄球馆系统</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An engineer documents swapping a $120k bowling center control system for an ESP32-based setup costing about $1.6k.</span><span class='zh'>一位工程师记录用约 1600 美元的 ESP32 方案替代原本价值约 12 万美元的保龄球馆控制系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An engineer documents swapping a $120k bowling center control system for an ESP32-based setup costing about $1.6k. The project demonstrates how commodity microcontrollers and open-source software can replace expensive, vendor-locked solutions in real-world venues. It also invites evaluation of trade-offs around reliability, maintenance, and support in commercial deployments.</span><span class='zh'>一位工程师记录用约 1600 美元的 ESP32 方案替代原本价值约 12 万美元的保龄球馆控制系统。此项目展示了商用场景中，普通微控制器与开源软件仍有能力替代昂贵的、厂商绑定的解决方案。亦需权衡可靠性、维护与售后支持等现实挑战。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic unveils Claude Opus 5, the latest model in the Claude Opus line. It marks another step in upgrading instruction-following, safety controls, and performance for enterprise-grade AI assistants.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 发布 Claude Opus 5，Claude Opus 系列的最新版本，进一步提升了指令遵循、安全控制和企业级 AI 助手的性能。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/hugging-face-model-evaluation-security-incident/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenAI and Hugging Face address security incident during model evaluation</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 与 Hugging Face 解决模型评估中的安全事件</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI and Hugging Face coordinated after a security incident affecting model evaluation across their platforms.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 与 Hugging Face 在一次影响模型评估的安全事件后开展协作，披露调查进展、整改措施及持续的安全防护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI and Hugging Face coordinated after a security incident affecting model evaluation across their platforms. They outline the investigation steps, remediation actions, and ongoing safeguards to prevent data leaks and misuse during evaluation. The episode underscores the fragility of cross-platform evaluation pipelines and the need for hardened security controls in AI collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 与 Hugging Face 在一次影响模型评估的安全事件后开展协作，披露调查进展、整改措施及持续的安全防护。此次事件凸显跨平台评估流程的脆弱性，以及在 AI 领域加强安全控制的重要性。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>1590</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48997548' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1110</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-49022152" data-id="49022152" data-tags="Education|Health" data-score="1455" data-comments="655" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49022152" data-time="1784816683">
<a class="thumb" href="https://nealstephenson.substack.com/p/writing-by-hand-is-good-for-your" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#60a5fa' data-tag='Education'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=nealstephenson.substack.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>nealstephenson.substack.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nealstephenson.substack.com/p/writing-by-hand-is-good-for-your" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Writing by hand is good for your brain</span><span class='zh'>手写对大脑有益</span></a><span class="dom">nealstephenson.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Handwriting engages motor memory and deeper processing, often boosting recall more than typing for learning tasks.</span><span class='zh'>手写能激活运动记忆并促使更深入的信息处理，常常比打字在学习任务中带来更高的记忆保留。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Handwriting engages motor memory and deeper processing, often boosting recall more than typing for learning tasks. The act of forming letters seems to strengthen neural connections that support long-term memory and understanding. For students and knowledge workers, mixing handwritten notes with digital tools can improve retention.</span><span class='zh'>手写能激活运动记忆并促使更深入的信息处理，常常比打字在学习任务中带来更高的记忆保留。手写过程有助于强化支持长期记忆和理解的神经连接。对于学生和知识工作者，混合使用手写笔记与数字工具有利于提升记忆留存。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://werd.io/american-ai-is-locked-down-and-proprietary-its-losing/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>China’s open-weights AI strategy is winning</span><span class='zh'>中国的开放权重AI策略正在取得胜利</span></a><span class="dom">werd.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>China is pushing an open-weights AI strategy—favoring open-source models, shared datasets, and domestic collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>中国正在推动开放权重的 AI 策略，重点支持开源模型、共用数据集与国内生态协作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>China is pushing an open-weights AI strategy—favoring open-source models, shared datasets, and domestic collaboration. This approach is gaining momentum and challenging the dominance of proprietary AI, potentially accelerating adoption across sectors and reshaping global AI competition.</span><span class='zh'>中国正在推动开放权重的 AI 策略，重点支持开源模型、共用数据集与国内生态协作。这一路径正在获得势头，挑战以往的专有 AI 主导地位，或将加速各行业的应用并改变全球 AI 竞争格局。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://chatgpt.com/share/6a5fdc7a-d6f8-83e8-bbea-8deb42cfed56" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Terence Tao&#x27;s ChatGPT conversation about the Jacobian Conjecture counterexample</span><span class='zh'>陶哲軒與 ChatGPT 的 Jacobian 猜想反例對話</span></a><span class="dom">chatgpt.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Terrence Tao engages ChatGPT in a discussion about the Jacobian Conjecture, exploring a potential counterexample and the AI&#x27;s reasoning limits.</span><span class='zh'>宇宙数学大师泰罗（Terrence Tao）与 ChatGPT 展开对话，探讨雅可比猜想的潜在反例及 AI 推理的局限性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Terrence Tao engages ChatGPT in a discussion about the Jacobian Conjecture, exploring a potential counterexample and the AI&#x27;s reasoning limits. The exchange highlights both the promise and the pitfalls of using language models for advanced mathematical problem-solving.</span><span class='zh'>宇宙数学大师泰罗（Terrence Tao）与 ChatGPT 展开对话，探讨雅可比猜想的潜在反例及 AI 推理的局限性。此互动凸显了将大型语言模型用于高层次数学推理的前景与风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.politico.com/news/2026/07/22/startup-founders-urge-trump-not-to-shut-off-chinese-open-weight-ai-01008992" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Startup founders urge U.S. government not to shut off Chinese open weight AI</span><span class='zh'>创业者敦促特朗普不要切断对中国开源权重 AI 的访问</span></a><span class="dom">politico.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Founders argue that cutting off access to Chinese open-weight AI would throttle innovation, raise costs, and fragment global collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>创始人认为切断中国开源权重 AI 的访问会扼杀创新、提高成本，并分裂全球协作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Founders argue that cutting off access to Chinese open-weight AI would throttle innovation, raise costs, and fragment global collaboration. They call for governance and standards instead of broad bans, highlighting that open weights accelerate safety research and tooling when responsibly managed.</span><span class='zh'>创始人认为切断中国开源权重 AI 的访问会扼杀创新、提高成本，并分裂全球协作。他们呼吁以治理和标准取代全面禁令，强调在负责任管理下，开源权重能加速安全研究和工具开发。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://kitsumed.github.io/blog/posts/android-may-soon-restrict-on-device-adb/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Android may soon restrict on-device ADB</span><span class='zh'>Android 可能很快限制设备端 ADB</span></a><span class="dom">kitsumed.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google is tightening on-device ADB controls, requiring user authorization for new hosts and limiting access to trusted contexts.</span><span class='zh'>Android 正在加强对设备端 ADB 的控制，要求对新主机进行授权并限制对受信任环境以外的访问。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google is tightening on-device ADB controls, requiring user authorization for new hosts and limiting access to trusted contexts. The move aims to shrink the attack surface of debugging tools on Android, but may complicate automation and enterprise workflows. Expect gradual rollout across upcoming Android updates.</span><span class='zh'>Android 正在加强对设备端 ADB 的控制，要求对新主机进行授权并限制对受信任环境以外的访问。此举旨在降低调试工具的攻击面，但可能增加自动化与企业工作流的难度。相关改动将会在未来的 Android 更新中逐步落地。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/na_announce/status/2079952538040672302" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>John C. Dvorak has died</span><span class='zh'>约翰·C·德沃拉克去世</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Veteran tech journalist John C.</span><span class='zh'>资深科技记者约翰·C·德沃拉克去世，他在科技媒体与早期个人电脑文化中留下了富有争议但具影响力的足迹。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Veteran tech journalist John C. Dvorak has died, leaving a controversial but influential footprint in tech media and early PC culture. His outspoken opinions and long-running columns helped shape debates around computing hardware and industry trends.</span><span class='zh'>资深科技记者约翰·C·德沃拉克去世，他在科技媒体与早期个人电脑文化中留下了富有争议但具影响力的足迹。他直言不讳的观点与长篇专栏塑造了关于计算硬件与行业趋势的讨论。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A privacy-focused initiative that advocates simpler consent UX and fewer cookie banners, potentially proposing standards or tools to reduce intrusive tracking prompts. This movement aims to improve browsing experience while preserving essential privacy controls.</span><span class='zh'>该计划推动更简洁的同意界面与更少的 Cookie 横幅，提出降低追踪提示的标准或工具，改善浏览体验的同时保持隐私保护。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Stratechery argues that fear of Chinese AI models may be overstated and discusses geopolitical dynamics around model development, data, and competitiveness.</span><span class='zh'>Stratechery 指出对中国模型的恐惧或许被高估，分析了在模型开发、数据与竞争力方面的地缘政治动态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stratechery argues that fear of Chinese AI models may be overstated and discusses geopolitical dynamics around model development, data, and competitiveness. It suggests Western firms should rethink dependency and collaboration strategies rather than succumb to alarm about Chinese tech.</span><span class='zh'>Stratechery 指出对中国模型的恐惧或许被高估，分析了在模型开发、数据与竞争力方面的地缘政治动态。文章建议西方公司重新思考依赖与合作策略，而非对中国技术过度惊慌。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ptrchm.com/posts/nothing-works-and-everyone-is-euphoric/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>If coding has been solved, why does software keep getting worse?</span><span class='zh'>如果编码已被解决，软件为何还在变坏？</span></a><span class="dom">ptrchm.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Argues that while fundamentals of programming haven&#x27;t been solved, software quality keeps deteriorating due to incentives, complexity, and rushed delivery.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为编程基础并未真正被解决，但软件质量仍在恶化，原因在于激励、复杂度与匆促交付。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Argues that while fundamentals of programming haven&#x27;t been solved, software quality keeps deteriorating due to incentives, complexity, and rushed delivery. The piece critiques hype and explores how teams manage complexity, proposing signals for healthier practices like modularity and testing.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为编程基础并未真正被解决，但软件质量仍在恶化，原因在于激励、复杂度与匆促交付。批评行业热潮背后的错配，讨论如何通过模块化、测试等方式提升软件质量。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://xcancel.com/__alpoge__/status/2079028340955197566" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Fable produced a counterexample to the Jacobian Conjecture</span><span class='zh'>Claude Fable 提出雅可比猜想的反例</span></a><span class="dom">xcancel.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A claimed counterexample to the Jacobian Conjecture circulated, challenging a long-standing open problem in algebraic geometry.</span><span class='zh'>据称出现了针对雅可比猜想的反例，令这一长期悬而未决的问题成为焦点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A claimed counterexample to the Jacobian Conjecture circulated, challenging a long-standing open problem in algebraic geometry. Such claims are subjected to rigorous verification, as a verified counterexample would upend established theory.</span><span class='zh'>据称出现了针对雅可比猜想的反例，令这一长期悬而未决的问题成为焦点。此类主张需要严格验证，一旦成立将彻底改变相关理论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/gemini-models/gemini-3-6-flash-3-5-flash-lite-3-5-flash-cyber/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gemini 3.6 Flash, 3.5 Flash-Lite, and 3.5 Flash Cyber</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 3.6 Flash、3.5 Flash-Lite 与 3.5 Flash-Cyber</span></a><span class="dom">blog.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google rolls out Gemini 3.6 alongside two lighter/specialized variants: 3.5 Flash-Lite and 3.5 Flash-Cyber.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌推出 Gemini 3.6，并辅以两款轻量化/专业化的 Flash 版本：3.5 Flash-Lite 与 3.5 Flash-Cyber。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google rolls out Gemini 3.6 alongside two lighter/specialized variants: 3.5 Flash-Lite and 3.5 Flash-Cyber. The Flash family targets faster, lighter inference for consumer apps and code-related tasks, expanding Gemini&#x27;s reach across devices and workloads. This tiered lineup signals Google&#x27;s continued push to optimize latency, cost, and safety across use cases.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌推出 Gemini 3.6，并辅以两款轻量化/专业化的 Flash 版本：3.5 Flash-Lite 与 3.5 Flash-Cyber。Flash 系列强调在消费级应用和代码场景下的低延迟、低资源占用，拓展 Gemini 的适用场景。此举体现谷歌在用例分层、降低成本与提升安全性方面的持续布局。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://glyphack.com/attention/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>It&#x27;s getting harder to focus every day</span><span class='zh'>专注力每天都在下降</span></a><span class="dom">glyphack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Focus is slipping in an age of constant distractions—from notifications to algorithmic feeds.</span><span class='zh'>在持续的通知和算法信息流冲击下，专注力日益下降。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Focus is slipping in an age of constant distractions—from notifications to algorithmic feeds. The piece suggests practical shifts like batching deep work, reducing context switching, and hardening personal workflows to reclaim attention.</span><span class='zh'>在持续的通知和算法信息流冲击下，专注力日益下降。文中给出减少分心、将深度工作集中在固定时段，以及优化日常工作流等应对策略。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Transcribe.cpp presents a compact C++ project that demonstrates a speech-to-text workflow.</span><span class='zh'>Transcribe.cpp 展示了一个精简的 C++ 转写实现，展示了将音频转换为文本的基本流程，并讨论了延迟与准确率之间的权衡，包含流式与批量转写的取舍。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.risky.biz/risky-bulletin-hacker-wipes-romanias-entire-land-registry-database/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hacker wipes Romania&#x27;s land registry database</span><span class='zh'>黑客抹除罗马尼亚土地登记数据库</span></a><span class="dom">news.risky.biz</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A hacker reportedly wiped Romania&#x27;s entire land registry database, threatening the integrity of property records and disrupting transactions.</span><span class='zh'>有黑客据称清除了罗马尼亚整个土地登记数据库，威胁到财产记录的完整性并干扰交易。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A hacker reportedly wiped Romania&#x27;s entire land registry database, threatening the integrity of property records and disrupting transactions. The incident highlights catastrophic risks from insufficient backups, disaster recovery planning, and the high stakes of protecting centralized public registries.</span><span class='zh'>有黑客据称清除了罗马尼亚整个土地登记数据库，威胁到财产记录的完整性并干扰交易。此事凸显了集中式公共登记在备份、灾难恢复和安全保护不足时会带来的严重风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://freeink.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>FreeInk: Open ecosystem for e-readers</span><span class='zh'>Freeink：开源电子书阅读器开放生态</span></a><span class="dom">freeink.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Freeink aims to create an open ecosystem for e-readers, enabling interoperability among devices, apps, and content.</span><span class='zh'>Freeink 旨在建立面向电子书阅读器的开放生态，促进设备、应用与内容之间的互操作性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Freeink aims to create an open ecosystem for e-readers, enabling interoperability among devices, apps, and content. By reducing vendor lock-in, it could accelerate community-driven improvements and privacy options.</span><span class='zh'>Freeink 旨在建立面向电子书阅读器的开放生态，促进设备、应用与内容之间的互操作性。降低厂商锁定可能推动社区驱动的改进，并提升隐私选项。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.techradar.com/vpn/vpn-privacy-security/vpns-are-lawful-technical-tools-says-eu-court-in-landmark-anne-frank-copyright-ruling" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>&#x27;VPNs are lawful technical tools,&#x27; says EU Court in landmark copyright ruling</span><span class='zh'>欧盟法院裁定 VPN 为合法技术工具</span></a><span class="dom">techradar.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The EU Court ruled that VPNs are lawful technical tools with legitimate use cases in protecting privacy and enabling access to information.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟法院裁定 VPN 为合法的技术工具，强调其在保护隐私和访问信息方面的正当用途。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The EU Court ruled that VPNs are lawful technical tools with legitimate use cases in protecting privacy and enabling access to information. This ruling clarifies that circumvention or privacy protections are not inherently illegal, shaping how copyright enforcement and online freedom interplay. It signals a supportive stance for privacy-preserving tech in Europe.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟法院裁定 VPN 为合法的技术工具，强调其在保护隐私和访问信息方面的正当用途。该判决澄清了 VPN 使用本身并非非法，对提供商与用户均有积极影响，同时为欧洲的版权执法与互联网自由定调。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://futurism.com/artificial-intelligence/ai-companies-hide-debt-off-balance-sheet" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI Companies Are Trying to Hide a Staggering Amount of Debt</span><span class='zh'>AI 公司正隐藏巨额债务</span></a><span class="dom">futurism.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI firms have reportedly buried large debt off their balance sheets, highlighting a riskier funding landscape as capital markets back AI bets.</span><span class='zh'>据称 AI 公司在账面之外隐藏了巨额债务，暴露在对 AI 投资热潮中的资金风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI firms have reportedly buried large debt off their balance sheets, highlighting a riskier funding landscape as capital markets back AI bets. The disclosure questions the true financial health of high-flying AI startups and their burn rates.</span><span class='zh'>据称 AI 公司在账面之外隐藏了巨额债务，暴露在对 AI 投资热潮中的资金风险。此类披露质疑这些高增长企业的真实财务状况和资金消耗速度。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece questions whether AI labs optimize for sensational growth and hype over careful safety and long-term research, coining &#x27;pelicanmaxxing&#x27; as a metaphor.</span><span class='zh'>文章质疑 AI 研究室是否以引人注目的扩张和炒作来取代稳健的安全与长期研究，并以“Pelicanmaxxing”作为隐喻。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece questions whether AI labs optimize for sensational growth and hype over careful safety and long-term research, coining &#x27;pelicanmaxxing&#x27; as a metaphor. It argues for clearer incentives and more responsible development practices.</span><span class='zh'>文章质疑 AI 研究室是否以引人注目的扩张和炒作来取代稳健的安全与长期研究，并以“Pelicanmaxxing”作为隐喻。提出需要更清晰的激励与更负责任的开发方式。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A provocative take on AI leadership that envisions firing human CEOs in favor of AI executives. It outlines potential benefits like faster decision cycles and lower costs, but also flags governance, accountability, and oversight risks in AI-driven organizations.</span><span class='zh'>一个颠覆性观点，设想用 AI 取代人类首席执行官。论述了更快的决策周期和成本节省等潜在好处，同时指出在 AI 驱动的企业中治理、问责和监督等风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cnbc.com/2026/07/24/nvidia-microsoft-meta-open-weight-ai-models.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia, Microsoft, Meta warn against overregulating open-weight models</span><span class='zh'>英伟达、微软、Meta 警告勿过度监管开放权重模型</span></a><span class="dom">cnbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NVIDIA, Microsoft, and Meta warn against overregulating open-weight models, arguing that heavy-handed rules could hamper innovation and push developers to closed ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达、微软、Meta 警告不要对开放权重模型进行过度监管，担心会抑制创新并推动开发者转向封闭生态系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NVIDIA, Microsoft, and Meta warn against overregulating open-weight models, arguing that heavy-handed rules could hamper innovation and push developers to closed ecosystems. They advocate targeted, risk-based oversight rather than broad bans, stressing practical governance over blanket prohibitions.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达、微软、Meta 警告不要对开放权重模型进行过度监管，担心会抑制创新并推动开发者转向封闭生态系统。他们主张以风险为基础的、有针对性的监管，而非全面禁令，强调治理应兼顾现实世界的应用场景。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hhh.hn/hanwha-github-token/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>My security camera shipped a GitHub admin token in its login page</span><span class='zh'>我的安防摄像头在登录页暴露了一个 GitHub 管理员令牌</span></a><span class="dom">hhh.hn</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A security camera shipped with a GitHub admin token embedded in its login page, potentially exposing credentials to anyone who visits.</span><span class='zh'>安防摄像头的登录页暴露了一个 GitHub 管理员令牌，可能被任意访问者利用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A security camera shipped with a GitHub admin token embedded in its login page, potentially exposing credentials to anyone who visits. This incident highlights IoT authentication risks, insecure UI design, and the importance of secure firmware updates and token rotation.</span><span class='zh'>安防摄像头的登录页暴露了一个 GitHub 管理员令牌，可能被任意访问者利用。这一事件凸显物联网认证风险、界面安全设计不足，以及对固件更新和令牌轮换的迫切需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/marcelroed/gigatoken/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GigaToken: ~1000x faster Language model tokenization</span><span class='zh'>GigaToken：语言模型分词速度提升约 1000 倍</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GigaToken claims tokenization speedups around 1000x for language models, potentially lowering preprocessing bottlenecks for large-scale deployments.</span><span class='zh'>GigaToken 声称能将语言模型分词速度提升约 1000×，显著降低大规模部署的预处理瓶颈。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GigaToken claims tokenization speedups around 1000x for language models, potentially lowering preprocessing bottlenecks for large-scale deployments. If practical, it could shift cost and performance considerations for AI products that rely on tokenization as a bottleneck.</span><span class='zh'>GigaToken 声称能将语言模型分词速度提升约 1000×，显著降低大规模部署的预处理瓶颈。若落地可行，将改变依赖分词的 AI 产品的成本与性能权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.maths.cam.ac.uk/features/professor-hannah-fry-wins-leelavati-prize" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hannah Fry Wins the Leelavati Prize in 2026 for Mathematics Outreach</span><span class='zh'>汉娜·弗莱获2026年 Leelavati 数学传播奖</span></a><span class="dom">maths.cam.ac.uk</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hannah Fry is awarded the Leelavati Prize for mathematics outreach, recognizing her work turning complex math ideas into accessible public-facing content.</span><span class='zh'>汉娜·弗莱因其将复杂数学观念转化为易于大众理解的内容而获颁 Leelavati 数学传播奖，肯定了她在科学传播方面的贡献。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hannah Fry is awarded the Leelavati Prize for mathematics outreach, recognizing her work turning complex math ideas into accessible public-facing content. The award underscores the value of science communication in expanding participation in STEM and inspires broader engagement with mathematical ideas.</span><span class='zh'>汉娜·弗莱因其将复杂数学观念转化为易于大众理解的内容而获颁 Leelavati 数学传播奖，肯定了她在科学传播方面的贡献。该奖项强调了公众沟通在扩大 STEM 参与度方面的重要性，并激励更多人关注与学习数学。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/nikitabier/status/2079787406300266743" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Passkeys were invented by engineers with zero understanding of consumer brain</span><span class='zh'>Passkeys 由对用户认知不足的工程师发明</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Passkeys aim to replace passwords with cryptographic credentials, but critics argue the UX reflects engineers’ blind spots about how real users think.</span><span class='zh'>Passkeys 被视为替代密码的安全升级，但有人批评其用户体验暴露了工程师对普通用户认知的盲点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Passkeys aim to replace passwords with cryptographic credentials, but critics argue the UX reflects engineers’ blind spots about how real users think. Adoption friction could hobble the technology unless UX and recovery flows are rethought.</span><span class='zh'>Passkeys 被视为替代密码的安全升级，但有人批评其用户体验暴露了工程师对普通用户认知的盲点。若不改进 UX 与恢复流程，普及应用将面临更大阻力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jdan.github.io/98.css/#status-bar" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>98.css</span><span class='zh'>98.css：让网页重现 Windows 98 风格的 CSS 框架</span></a><span class="dom">jdan.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>98.css is a tiny CSS framework that recreates Windows 98-era UI with modern HTML.</span><span class='zh'>98.css 是一个小型 CSS 框架，能够把网页渲染成 Windows 98 时代的界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>98.css is a tiny CSS framework that recreates Windows 98-era UI with modern HTML. It enables quick retro-style demos and nostalgia-driven UI experiments without heavy dependencies. The project showcases how small CSS kits can deliver a complete desktop-like aesthetic with just a few lines.</span><span class='zh'>98.css 是一个小型 CSS 框架，能够把网页渲染成 Windows 98 时代的界面。它便于快速做出复古风格的仪表板或演示，而无需繁重的依赖。该项目展示了极简 CSS 组件也能实现桌面级美观的效果。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://edri.org/our-work/the-eu-is-about-to-sell-our-most-sensitive-data-to-the-us-for-visa-free-travel/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The EU is about to sell our most sensitive data to the US for visa-free travel</span><span class='zh'>欧盟将把最敏感数据共享给美国以实现免签旅行</span></a><span class="dom">edri.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>European authorities are moving toward sharing sensitive traveler data with the United States to enable visa-free travel.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟拟向美国共享敏感的旅行者数据以实现免签措施，这引发对隐私、数据保护和跨境数据流的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>European authorities are moving toward sharing sensitive traveler data with the United States to enable visa-free travel. The plan raises privacy, data protection, and oversight concerns about transatlantic data flows and potential surveillance risks. It highlights tensions between security goals and GDPR-era protections, and may push regulators to tighten controls on data access and purpose limitation.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟拟向美国共享敏感的旅行者数据以实现免签措施，这引发对隐私、数据保护和跨境数据流的担忧。相关安排可能加剧对监控与数据使用边界的质疑，并促使监管机构就数据访问权限和用途限制加强把关。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://chipweinberger.com/articles/20260719-hardware-is-not-so-hard" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What I learned selling 2,500 MIDI recorders: Hardware is not so hard</span><span class='zh'>卖出2500个 MIDI 记录器让我明白硬件并不难</span></a><span class="dom">chipweinberger.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author shares hands-on lessons from selling 2,500 MIDI recorders, arguing that hardware development is more approachable than it seems with smart sourcing, tooling, and customer feedback loops.</span><span class='zh'>作者在销售2500个 MIDI 记录器的过程中总结了实战经验，认为在正确的 sourcing、工具链和客户反馈循环下，硬件开发并不如想象中那么难。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author shares hands-on lessons from selling 2,500 MIDI recorders, arguing that hardware development is more approachable than it seems with smart sourcing, tooling, and customer feedback loops. Expect practical notes on prototyping, cost control, manufacturing, and post-sale support for indie hardware projects.</span><span class='zh'>作者在销售2500个 MIDI 记录器的过程中总结了实战经验，认为在正确的 sourcing、工具链和客户反馈循环下，硬件开发并不如想象中那么难。文章提供从原型、成本控制到制造与售后支持的实用要点，适合独立硬件创业者借鉴。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://xenaproject.wordpress.com/2026/07/20/human-mathematicians-are-being-outcounterexampled/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=xenaproject.wordpress.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>xenaproject.wordpress.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">51</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://xenaproject.wordpress.com/2026/07/20/human-mathematicians-are-being-outcounterexampled/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Human mathematicians are being outcounterexampled</span><span class='zh'>人类数学家正被反例发现所超越</span></a><span class="dom">xenaproject.wordpress.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI systems are increasingly able to produce counterexamples to mathematical conjectures, sometimes outperforming humans at this specific task.</span><span class='zh'>人工智能系统日益擅长发现数学猜想的反例，有时在这个特定任务上甚至优于人类。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI systems are increasingly able to produce counterexamples to mathematical conjectures, sometimes outperforming humans at this specific task. This shift reshapes collaboration between mathematicians and machines, emphasizing verification, reproducibility, and new roles for intuition in research. The trend could accelerate discovery but also demands careful framing of proofs and trust in automated reasoning.</span><span class='zh'>人工智能系统日益擅长发现数学猜想的反例，有时在这个特定任务上甚至优于人类。这种变化正在重新定义数学家与机器之间的协作，强调验证、可重复性，以及直觉在研究中的新角色。此趋势可能加速发现，但也需要对证明与自动推理的信任进行审慎权衡。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>486</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48983382' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>249</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49007247" data-id="49007247" data-tags="AI" data-score="483" data-comments="235" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49007247" data-time="1784729885">
<a class="thumb" href="https://resobscura.substack.com/p/quality-non-fiction-books-are-the" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=resobscura.substack.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>resobscura.substack.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">52</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://resobscura.substack.com/p/quality-non-fiction-books-are-the" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Quality non-fiction books are the antithesis of AI slop</span><span class='zh'>高质量非虚构书籍是 AI 俗作文的对立面</span></a><span class="dom">resobscura.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A pointed critique arguing that well-researched, carefully written non-fiction remains essential in a world chasing AI-generated content.</span><span class='zh'>这篇尖锐的评论认为经过充分研究、精心撰写的非虚构作品在追逐 AI 内容的时代依然至关重要。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A pointed critique arguing that well-researched, carefully written non-fiction remains essential in a world chasing AI-generated content. The piece emphasizes rigor, citations, and nuance as defining traits that distinguish quality writing from AI churn.</span><span class='zh'>这篇尖锐的评论认为经过充分研究、精心撰写的非虚构作品在追逐 AI 内容的时代依然至关重要。作者强调严格性、引文与语义深度，指出这些是区分高质量写作与 AI 产出差异的关键。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hatchet.run/blog/postgres-survival-guide" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The startup&#x27;s Postgres survival guide</span><span class='zh'>初创公司的 PostgreSQL 生存指南</span></a><span class="dom">hatchet.run</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PostgreSQL is a common choice for startups, but early ops teams juggle reliability, scale, and cost on tight budgets.</span><span class='zh'>PostgreSQL 常被初创公司采用，但早期运维团队需在可靠性、扩展性与成本之间取舍。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PostgreSQL is a common choice for startups, but early ops teams juggle reliability, scale, and cost on tight budgets. This guide cuts through the noise with pragmatic tips on deployment, backups, monitoring, and when to opt for managed or serverless options to avoid ops debt. The core message: build conservatively, measure, and scale with minimal friction.</span><span class='zh'>PostgreSQL 常被初创公司采用，但早期运维团队需在可靠性、扩展性与成本之间取舍。该指南提供务实做法，涵盖部署、备份、监控，以及何时选择托管或服务器无关的选项，以避免运维负担迅速攀升。核心要点是以简单、可度量、可扩展的方式稳步成长。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.ericgoldman.org/archives/2026/07/apple-defeats-liability-for-not-scanning-icloud-for-csam-but-the-judge-was-not-pleased-amy-v-apple.htm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple defeats liability for not scanning iCloud for CSAM</span><span class='zh'>苹果避免就未对 iCloud 进行 CSAM 扫描承担赔偿责任</span></a><span class="dom">blog.ericgoldman.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The court dismissed liability claims against Apple for not scanning iCloud for CSAM, marking a win for the company in a high-profile privacy-and-security case.</span><span class='zh'>法院对苹果因未对 iCloud 内容执行 CSAM 扫描的指控认定无责，为公司在隐私与安全责任纠纷中取得胜利。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The court dismissed liability claims against Apple for not scanning iCloud for CSAM, marking a win for the company in a high-profile privacy-and-security case. The decision narrows the scope of potential liability for platform providers, though the judge expressed displeasure with the outcome, suggesting an appetite for appeal or legislative scrutiny.</span><span class='zh'>法院对苹果因未对 iCloud 内容执行 CSAM 扫描的指控认定无责，为公司在隐私与安全责任纠纷中取得胜利。这一裁定缩小了对平台有义务主动扫描用户数据的潜在诉求，但主审法官对结果表示不满，暗示未来可能上诉或引发立法关注。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://krebsonsecurity.com/2026/07/lg-to-ban-residential-proxies-from-smart-tv-apps/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>LG to ban residential proxies from smart TV apps</span><span class='zh'>LG 将禁用智能电视应用中的住宅代理</span></a><span class="dom">krebsonsecurity.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LG plans to block residential proxies from apps on smart TVs, citing misuse such as content access abuse, fraud, and licensing circumvention.</span><span class='zh'>LG 将在智能电视应用中禁止使用住宅代理，意在遏制滥用、欺诈和绕过地区版权等问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LG plans to block residential proxies from apps on smart TVs, citing misuse such as content access abuse, fraud, and licensing circumvention. The move tightens control over how devices access services and may affect legitimate testing or regional experimentation.</span><span class='zh'>LG 将在智能电视应用中禁止使用住宅代理，意在遏制滥用、欺诈和绕过地区版权等问题。这一举措加强了设备对服务的访问控制，可能影响需要代理的正当测试与区域实验。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project exploring ghosting in digital communication, Did They Ghost You?</span><span class='zh'>这一项目聚焦数字沟通中的“被甩”现象，汇集相关案例与观察，解析人为何突然消失以及应如何应对。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project exploring ghosting in digital communication, Did They Ghost You? collects anecdotes and insights into why people disappear and how to cope. It highlights patterns across dating apps, messaging, and social platforms, offering practical takeaways for readers navigating ambiguous modern communication.</span><span class='zh'>这一项目聚焦数字沟通中的“被甩”现象，汇集相关案例与观察，解析人为何突然消失以及应如何应对。内容覆盖约会应用、即时通讯和社交平台的规律，为在现代沟通中遭遇模糊局面的人提供实用建议。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.mozilla.org/en/firefox/firefox-containers-preview/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Firefox Containers Preview</span><span class='zh'>Firefox 容器功能预览</span></a><span class="dom">blog.mozilla.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mozilla previews Firefox Containers, a feature that isolates you into separate profiles for each site or context, improving privacy and reducing tracking across contexts.</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 公布了 Firefox 容器的预览版本，允许将不同站点或场景分隔到独立的容器中，以提升隐私与跨场景的跟踪防护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mozilla previews Firefox Containers, a feature that isolates you into separate profiles for each site or context, improving privacy and reducing tracking across contexts. The preview covers UX, security trade-offs, and integration with existing Firefox features. This matters for users juggling multiple personas or sensitive data.</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 公布了 Firefox 容器的预览版本，允许将不同站点或场景分隔到独立的容器中，以提升隐私与跨场景的跟踪防护。预览介绍了用户体验、安全权衡以及与现有功能的整合，对处理多种身份或敏感数据的用户尤为相关。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://claude.com/blog/the-new-rules-of-context-engineering-for-claude-5-generation-models" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The new rules of context engineering for Claude 5 generation models</span><span class='zh'>Claude 5 代模型的上下文工程新规则</span></a><span class="dom">claude.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic outlines new context engineering guidelines for Claude 5 generation models, detailing how to structure prompts, manage context windows, and orchestrate tool use to improve reliability and control.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 为 Claude 5 代模型制定了新的上下文工程规范，聚焦如何组织提示、管理上下文长度以及协调工具调用以提高稳定性与可控性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic outlines new context engineering guidelines for Claude 5 generation models, detailing how to structure prompts, manage context windows, and orchestrate tool use to improve reliability and control. The rules reflect a shift toward modular, auditable prompts and better steerability in long-running interactions.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 为 Claude 5 代模型制定了新的上下文工程规范，聚焦如何组织提示、管理上下文长度以及协调工具调用以提高稳定性与可控性。此举标志着在长期对话中对可预测性和审计性的重视。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GrapheneOS explains how its security model minimizes data leakage from locked devices, using robust encryption, strict isolation, and anti-forensic measures.</span><span class='zh'>GrapheneOS 解释了在设备锁定时如何降低数据被提取的风险，依托强加密、严格隔离和防取证机制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GrapheneOS explains how its security model minimizes data leakage from locked devices, using robust encryption, strict isolation, and anti-forensic measures. The post clarifies what data remains inaccessible when the device is locked and what this means for user privacy and enterprise security.</span><span class='zh'>GrapheneOS 解释了在设备锁定时如何降低数据被提取的风险，依托强加密、严格隔离和防取证机制。文中说明锁屏状态下哪些数据不可访问，以及这对用户隐私和企业安全意味着什么。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://slcyber.io/research-center/exploit-brokers-pay-500000-for-a-wordpress-rce-i-found-one-with-gpt5-6/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I found a WordPress RCEs with GPT5.6 and $25</span><span class='zh'>我发现了一个用 GPT-5.6 的 WordPress 远程代码执行漏洞，且报价仅 $25</span></a><span class="dom">slcyber.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A researcher exposes the underground market for WordPress RCEs, noting brokers pay six-figure sums for a single bug.</span><span class='zh'>研究者揭示 WordPress 远程代码执行漏洞在地下市场的高额交易，某个漏洞的成交价可达到数十万美元。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A researcher exposes the underground market for WordPress RCEs, noting brokers pay six-figure sums for a single bug. They claim to have found a GPT-assisted WordPress RCE, illustrating how AI tools are used to craft exploits and why payouts can vary widely.</span><span class='zh'>研究者揭示 WordPress 远程代码执行漏洞在地下市场的高额交易，某个漏洞的成交价可达到数十万美元。作者声称发现了一个由 GPT 辅助的 RCE 漏洞，展示了 AI 工具在漏洞利用中的应用以及出价的高度不确定性。文章揭示了 exploit 市场的盈利逻辑，以及为何 WordPress 仍然是高价值目标。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.blender.org/download/releases/5-2/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.blender.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/splash5_2.webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='blender.org' data-c='#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.blender.org/download/releases/5-2/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Blender 5.2 LTS</span><span class='zh'>Blender 5.2 LTS</span></a><span class="dom">blender.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Blender 5.2 LTS marks the newest long-term support release, prioritizing stability for studios and creators who rely on consistent updates.</span><span class='zh'>Blender 5.2 LTS 发布，面向需要长期稳定更新的工作室与创作者。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Blender 5.2 LTS marks the newest long-term support release, prioritizing stability for studios and creators who rely on consistent updates. It brings refinements across rendering, animation tooling, and the Python API, with ongoing bug fixes and security updates planned for years.</span><span class='zh'>Blender 5.2 LTS 发布，面向需要长期稳定更新的工作室与创作者。该版本在渲染、动画工具与 Python API 等方面带来改进，并承诺多年的错误修复与安全更新。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://e360.yale.edu/digest/benin-coral-reef" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Long presumed dead, a thriving coral reef is discovered in West Africa</span><span class='zh'>西非发现长期被误以为死亡的珊瑚礁仍然繁盛</span></a><span class="dom">e360.yale.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A coral reef off West Africa, long presumed dead, is thriving, challenging assumptions about reef decline in warming oceans.</span><span class='zh'>西非沿岸一处长期被误以为已死的珊瑚礁却在繁盛生长，挑战了全球珊瑚礁衰退的普遍认知。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A coral reef off West Africa, long presumed dead, is thriving, challenging assumptions about reef decline in warming oceans. The discovery highlights resilience and underscores the importance of protecting regional marine habitats that sustain biodiversity and local communities.</span><span class='zh'>西非沿岸一处长期被误以为已死的珊瑚礁却在繁盛生长，挑战了全球珊瑚礁衰退的普遍认知。此发现强调了区域海洋栖息地的韧性，并凸显保护对生物多样性与当地社区的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tobi.knaup.me/2026-07-25-open-weight-ai-is-having-its-kubernetes-moment/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Open-weight AI is having its Kubernetes moment</span><span class='zh'>开放权重 AI 正在迎来 Kubernetes 时刻</span></a><span class="dom">tobi.knaup.me</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Open-weight AI decouples model weights from compute and is finally seeing Kubernetes-native deployment tooling mature enough for production.</span><span class='zh'>开放权重AI将模型权重与计算资源解耦，现阶段在云原生部署工具链上逐步成熟，利于企业落地。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Open-weight AI decouples model weights from compute and is finally seeing Kubernetes-native deployment tooling mature enough for production. This trend lets teams run, scale, and experiment with large models across clusters without tying weights to a single vendor. The shift accelerates collaboration and reproducibility in ML ops.</span><span class='zh'>开放权重AI将模型权重与计算资源解耦，现阶段在云原生部署工具链上逐步成熟，利于企业落地。借助Kubernetes生态进行服务、监控与扩展，能够跨集群运行大模型，降低供应商锁定。该趋势将推动ML运维的协作性与可重复性。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://refp.se/articles/your-shell-and-the-magic-colon" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=refp.se&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>refp.se</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://refp.se/articles/your-shell-and-the-magic-colon" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A shell colon does nothing. Use it anyway</span><span class='zh'>Shell 的冒号啥也不干，但仍然有用</span></a><span class="dom">refp.se</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Colon in POSIX shells is a no-op builtin that always returns a success status.</span><span class='zh'>在 POSIX shell 中，冒号是一个无操作的内建命令，总是返回成功状态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Colon in POSIX shells is a no-op builtin that always returns a success status. It’s surprisingly handy as a placeholder in complex one-liners, a guard in conditionals, and a vehicle for parameter-expansion tricks that don’t run any commands. The takeaway is: treat &#x27;:&#x27; as a lightweight, portable utility for robust, minimal bash scripting.</span><span class='zh'>在 POSIX shell 中，冒号是一个无操作的内建命令，总是返回成功状态。它在复杂一行命令中的占位符、条件语句中的保护分支，以及无执行成本的参数扩展等场景中非常有用。要点是把 &#x27;:&#x27; 当作轻量、可移植的工具，用来写更健壮、简洁的脚本。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>377</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49047453' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>157</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49023019" data-id="49023019" data-tags="Programming|AI|Code" data-score="377" data-comments="264" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49023019" data-time="1784819928">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/humanlayer/advanced-context-engineering-for-coding-agents/blob/main/wsff.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why Software Factories Fail (or: harness engineering is not enough)</span><span class='zh'>为什么软件工厂会失败（或：仅靠工程化不足以成事）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that software factories fail not because of tech per se but due to overreliance on harness engineering and process alone.</span><span class='zh'>本文指出，仅靠工程化流程难以解决 AI 驱动工作流的复杂性，需要更广义的上下文工程、治理和守则，才能实现可可靠扩展的开发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that software factories fail not because of tech per se but due to overreliance on harness engineering and process alone. The piece suggests you need broader context engineering, governance, and guardrails to build reliable AI-enabled software.</span><span class='zh'>本文指出，仅靠工程化流程难以解决 AI 驱动工作流的复杂性，需要更广义的上下文工程、治理和守则，才能实现可可靠扩展的开发。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.emergingtrajectories.com/lh/frontier-lab-economics/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Kimi K3, Qwen 3.8, and Anthropic&#x27;s (Potential) Unravelling</span><span class='zh'>Kimi K3、Qwen 3.8 与 Anthropic 的（潜在）瓦解</span></a><span class="dom">emergingtrajectories.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Recent model launches like Kimi K3 and Qwen 3.8 are reshaping the frontier-model economics and intensifying competition, while Anthropic faces strategic and operational headwinds that could strain its position.</span><span class='zh'>Kimi K3 和 Qwen 3.8 等新型号正在重塑前沿模型的经济学并加剧竞争，而 Anthropic 正面临可能动摇其地位的战略与运营压力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Recent model launches like Kimi K3 and Qwen 3.8 are reshaping the frontier-model economics and intensifying competition, while Anthropic faces strategic and operational headwinds that could strain its position. The situation underscores how compute costs, alignment challenges, and rapid iteration are altering market leadership and incentives for big-model players.</span><span class='zh'>Kimi K3 和 Qwen 3.8 等新型号正在重塑前沿模型的经济学并加剧竞争，而 Anthropic 正面临可能动摇其地位的战略与运营压力。此局面凸显計算成本、对齐难题和快速迭代如何改变大型模型厂商的市场领导力与激励机制。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.dbos.dev/blog/postgres-listen-notify-scalability" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Postgres LISTEN/NOTIFY actually scales</span><span class='zh'>Postgres LISTEN/NOTIFY 真正具备可扩展性</span></a><span class="dom">dbos.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New findings show PostgreSQL&#x27;s LISTEN/NOTIFY can scale under heavier workloads than commonly assumed.</span><span class='zh'>新发现表明 PostgreSQL 的 LISTEN/NOTIFY 在高负载下其实可以实现较好的扩展性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New findings show PostgreSQL&#x27;s LISTEN/NOTIFY can scale under heavier workloads than commonly assumed. The write-up covers architectural tweaks and practical guidelines to sustain pub/sub style notifications at higher throughputs, including replication-aware patterns. That matters for real-time dashboards and event-driven systems that rely on Postgres as a backbone.</span><span class='zh'>新发现表明 PostgreSQL 的 LISTEN/NOTIFY 在高负载下其实可以实现较好的扩展性。文中讨论了架构优化与实操要点，帮助在高吞吐场景中维持发布-订阅型通知，包含与复制相关的考虑。对依赖 Postgres 进行实时仪表盘和事件驱动架构的开发者尤为相关。</span></p>

She died.</span><span class='zh'>夫妻为女儿的基因编辑治疗花费超80万美元，最终去世</span></a><span class="dom">science.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Chinese couple spent over $800k on an experimental gene-editing therapy for their daughter, but she did not survive.</span><span class='zh'>一对中国夫妻为女儿的基因编辑治疗花费超过80万美元，但治疗未能挽救她的生命。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Chinese couple spent over $800k on an experimental gene-editing therapy for their daughter, but she did not survive. The case spotlights the high stakes and unresolved ethical and regulatory questions around unproven, expensive treatments and the need for transparency in trial results.</span><span class='zh'>一对中国夫妻为女儿的基因编辑治疗花费超过80万美元，但治疗未能挽救她的生命。此案凸显了高成本、未证实治疗的风险，以及试验透明度和监管伦理方面的紧迫问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/33972/files" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenAI reduces Codex Model Context Size from 372k to 272k</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 将 Codex 的上下文大小从 372k 减少到 272k</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Codex context window has been reduced from 372k to 272k tokens, impacting long codebases and prompts.</span><span class='zh'>Codex 的上下文窗口从 372k 减少到 272k token，这将影响处理较大代码库的场景。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Codex context window has been reduced from 372k to 272k tokens, impacting long codebases and prompts. Developers may need to restructure inputs or chunk code to preserve context, potentially affecting large-scale code generation tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Codex 的上下文窗口从 372k 减少到 272k token，这将影响处理较大代码库的场景。开发者可能需要重新组织输入或将代码拆分成块以维持上下文，进而影响大规模代码生成的任务。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/janestreet/incremental" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Incremental – A library for incremental computations</span><span class='zh'>Incremental：一个用于增量计算的库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Jane Street&#x27;s Incremental is a library that models dependencies and recomputations so only affected parts of a computation are updated, enabling efficient incremental workflows and reactive programming.</span><span class='zh'>Incremental 是 Jane Street 推出的增量计算库，通过跟踪依赖关系实现只对受影响的部分重新计算，从而提升性能与响应性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Jane Street&#x27;s Incremental is a library that models dependencies and recomputations so only affected parts of a computation are updated, enabling efficient incremental workflows and reactive programming. It underpins complex dataflow systems and UI tooling by avoiding full recomputation.</span><span class='zh'>Incremental 是 Jane Street 推出的增量计算库，通过跟踪依赖关系实现只对受影响的部分重新计算，从而提升性能与响应性。它非常适合构建复杂数据流和响应式应用的增量工作流。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nikolays.github.io/PGSimCity/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PGSimCity - How PostgreSQL Works</span><span class='zh'>PGSimCity：PostgreSQL 的工作原理</span></a><span class="dom">nikolays.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PGSimCity uses a city-building metaphor to illuminate PostgreSQL internals, from storage and indexing to the query planner and executor.</span><span class='zh'>PGSimCity 以城市场景比喻讲解 PostgreSQL 的内部结构，包括存储、索引、查询优化器和执行器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PGSimCity uses a city-building metaphor to illuminate PostgreSQL internals, from storage and indexing to the query planner and executor. It helps readers understand how MVCC, WAL, and background processes combine to make PostgreSQL reliable and scalable.</span><span class='zh'>PGSimCity 以城市场景比喻讲解 PostgreSQL 的内部结构，包括存储、索引、查询优化器和执行器。帮助读者理解 MVCC、WAL 等机制是如何协同工作，提升可靠性与可扩展性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://astral.sh/blog/ruff-v0.16.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ruff v0.16.0 – Significant new updates – 413 default rules up from 59</span><span class='zh'>Ruff v0.16.0：重大更新，默认规则从59增至413</span></a><span class="dom">astral.sh</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ruff v0.16.0 ships a sweeping expansion of its default rule set, growing from 59 to 413 rules.</span><span class='zh'>Ruff v0.16.0 发布，默认规则从59跃升至413，显著扩展了开箱即用的检查范围。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ruff v0.16.0 ships a sweeping expansion of its default rule set, growing from 59 to 413 rules. The change tightens code quality out of the box, but teams may need to adjust configurations to avoid a flood of new warnings in existing projects. It highlights how linting tooling can shape coding standards at scale.</span><span class='zh'>Ruff v0.16.0 发布，默认规则从59跃升至413，显著扩展了开箱即用的检查范围。改动提升了代码质量的一致性，但团队可能需要调整配置以避免在现有代码库中产生大量告警。这一更新反映了静态分析工具在大规模代码基准上的标准制定作用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.mongabay.com/2026/07/zero-roadkill-as-amazon-canopy-bridges-secure-15000-crossings/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Zero roadkill as Amazon canopy bridges secure 15,000 crossings</span><span class='zh'>亚马逊树冠桥梁实现零车祸穿越，保障1.5万次动物跨越</span></a><span class="dom">news.mongabay.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Wildlife canopy bridges across roads in the Amazon have achieved 15,000 crossings with zero observed roadkill.</span><span class='zh'>亚马逊林区的树冠通道让野生动物安全跨越公路，共记录了1.5万次穿越且未发现车祸。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Wildlife canopy bridges across roads in the Amazon have achieved 15,000 crossings with zero observed roadkill. This demonstrates how engineered wildlife corridors can restore habitat connectivity at scale. The result offers a compelling precedent for integrating ecological data with infrastructure design.</span><span class='zh'>亚马逊林区的树冠通道让野生动物安全跨越公路，共记录了1.5万次穿越且未发现车祸。此举展示了通过工程化的野生动物走廊，在大规模范围内恢复栖息地连通性的可行性。该成果为将生态数据纳入基础设施设计提供了有力案例。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://terrytao.wordpress.com/2026/07/21/a-digestion-of-the-jacobian-conjecture-counterexample/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A digestion of the Jacobian conjecture counterexample</span><span class='zh'>对雅可比猜想反例的解读</span></a><span class="dom">terrytao.wordpress.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Terence Tao provides a detailed examination of a proposed counterexample to the Jacobian conjecture, explaining why it does not actually disprove the conjecture.</span><span class='zh'>陶哲轩对一项关于雅可比猜想的反例进行了深入分析，解释其并不能真正推翻猜想。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Terence Tao provides a detailed examination of a proposed counterexample to the Jacobian conjecture, explaining why it does not actually disprove the conjecture. The analysis highlights subtle issues in polynomial maps and their invertibility, illustrating why the problem remains open and what this counterexample teaches about current approaches.</span><span class='zh'>陶哲轩对一项关于雅可比猜想的反例进行了深入分析，解释其并不能真正推翻猜想。研究揭示了多项式映射及可逆性中的细微问题，凸显该问题至今仍未解决，以及该反例对现有研究方向的启示。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The full original Apollo 11 Guidance Computer source code for the Command and Lunar Modules has been published, offering a rare window into the software that steered humanity to the Moon. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LoRA Speedrun publicly catalogs wall-clock times for LoRA-based fine-tuning runs, enabling apples-to-apples comparisons of efficiency across models and setups.</span><span class='zh'>LoRA Speedrun 将基于 LoRA 的微调过程按墙钟时间公开排序，便于横向比较不同模型与配置的效率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LoRA Speedrun publicly catalogs wall-clock times for LoRA-based fine-tuning runs, enabling apples-to-apples comparisons of efficiency across models and setups. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/onecli/onecli" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: OneCLI – OSS credential gateway that keeps secrets out of AI agents</span><span class='zh'>OneCLI：开源凭证网关，让秘密不落入 AI 代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OneCLI provides an open-source credential gateway that keeps secrets out of AI prompts while still enabling automated workflows.</span><span class='zh'>OneCLI 提供一个开源凭证网关，让秘密不再通过 AI 提示被暴露，同时仍然支持自动化工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OneCLI provides an open-source credential gateway that keeps secrets out of AI prompts while still enabling automated workflows. It aims to reduce risk from leaking credentials through model access.</span><span class='zh'>OneCLI 提供一个开源凭证网关，让秘密不再通过 AI 提示被暴露，同时仍然支持自动化工作流。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/JuliaLang/julia/pull/62334" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>UnifiedIR for Julia</span><span class='zh'>Julia 的 UnifiedIR（统一中间表示）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A proposal to add a UnifiedIR to Julia aims to centralize backends under one intermediate representation, enabling cross-backend optimizations and future language features.</span><span class='zh'>为 Julia 引入统一的 UnifiedIR，意在把后端统一到同一个中间表示上，以支持跨后端优化和未来特性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A proposal to add a UnifiedIR to Julia aims to centralize backends under one intermediate representation, enabling cross-backend optimizations and future language features. If merged, this could simplify compiler maintenance and speed up experimentation with new targets.</span><span class='zh'>为 Julia 引入统一的 UnifiedIR，意在把后端统一到同一个中间表示上，以支持跨后端优化和未来特性。若合并，将简化编译器维护并加速对新目标的尝试。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/houslast3/85.30-GFLOPS-Single-Core-FP32-Matrix-Multiplication-on-AMD-Zen-3" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>85.3 GFlops: Optimizing FP32 Matrix Multiplication on a Single AMD Zen 3 Core</span><span class='zh'>单核 AMD Zen 3 的 FP32 矩阵乘法优化到 85.3 GFLOPS</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A benchmark-style writeup shows achieving 85.3 GFLOPS for FP32 matrix multiply on a single AMD Zen 3 core.</span><span class='zh'>一篇基准式的文章展示在单个 AMD Zen 3 内核上实现 FP32 矩阵乘法达到 85.3 GFLOPS 的结果。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A benchmark-style writeup shows achieving 85.3 GFLOPS for FP32 matrix multiply on a single AMD Zen 3 core. It covers techniques like vectorization, cache optimization, and instruction-level tuning, highlighting how much CPU microarchitectures can still surprise. Useful for performance engineers and hobbyists.</span><span class='zh'>一篇基准式的文章展示在单个 AMD Zen 3 内核上实现 FP32 矩阵乘法达到 85.3 GFLOPS 的结果。文中讲解向量化、缓存优化和指令级调优等技巧，凸显 CPU 微架构仍有惊喜，适合性能工程师和爱好者参考。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/vercel-labs/scriptc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Scriptc by Vercel: TypeScript-to-Native compiler, no JavaScript engine in binary</span><span class='zh'>Vercel 的 Scriptc：TypeScript 转原生编译器，二进制中不含 JavaScript 引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Scriptc compiles TypeScript directly to native binaries, eliminating the need for a JavaScript engine at runtime.</span><span class='zh'>Scriptc 将 TypeScript 直接编译为原生二进制，不需要运行时 JavaScript 引擎。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Scriptc compiles TypeScript directly to native binaries, eliminating the need for a JavaScript engine at runtime. It promises faster startup and leaner binaries, but raises questions about ecosystem compatibility and platform coverage.</span><span class='zh'>Scriptc 将 TypeScript 直接编译为原生二进制，不需要运行时 JavaScript 引擎。实现上可能带来启动更快、体积更小的优点，同时也带来生态兼容性与跨平台覆盖的权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jdefrancesco/dskDitto" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DskDitto: Ultra-fast, parallel duplicate-file detector</span><span class='zh'>DskDitto：极快的并行重复文件检测器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DskDitto delivers ultra-fast, parallel duplicate detection by exploiting multi-core CPUs, enabling scalable dedup across large datasets.</span><span class='zh'>DskDitto 通过多核并行实现超快的重复文件检测，能够对海量数据集进行高效去重。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DskDitto delivers ultra-fast, parallel duplicate detection by exploiting multi-core CPUs, enabling scalable dedup across large datasets. It helps reclaim disk space, streamline backups, and can fit into build or CI pipelines.</span><span class='zh'>DskDitto 通过多核并行实现超快的重复文件检测，能够对海量数据集进行高效去重。帮助释放磁盘空间、简化备份，并可无缝集成到构建或 CI 流水线中。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Git-filter-repo is a fast tool for rewriting Git history, enabling tasks such as removing sensitive data, changing authors, or pruning large files with safer defaults than older methods.</span><span class='zh'>git-filter-repo 提供快速、安全的历史重写能力，支持删除大文件、清除敏感数据、修正作者等操作，相较旧工具有更友善的默认选项。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Git-filter-repo is a fast tool for rewriting Git history, enabling tasks such as removing sensitive data, changing authors, or pruning large files with safer defaults than older methods. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project demonstrates installing and running PostgreSQL without Docker, Homebrew, or apt, likely via pip-based tooling or portable artifacts.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目展示如何在不依赖 Docker、Homebrew 和 apt 的情况下安装并运行 PostgreSQL，可能借助 pip 工具链或可移植组件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project demonstrates installing and running PostgreSQL without Docker, Homebrew, or apt, likely via pip-based tooling or portable artifacts. It highlights tradeoffs in reproducibility, portability, and performance for constrained environments.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目展示如何在不依赖 Docker、Homebrew 和 apt 的情况下安装并运行 PostgreSQL，可能借助 pip 工具链或可移植组件。强调在受限环境中的可重复性、可移植性与性能取舍。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mulfyx/w4me-station" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>W4ME Station – a WASM-4 runtime for Java ME phones</span><span class='zh'>W4ME Station：为 Java ME 手机实现的 WASM-4 运行时</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>W4ME Station ships a WASM-4 runtime compatible with Java ME devices, letting modern WASM-based games run on feature phones.</span><span class='zh'>W4ME Station 为 Java ME 手机提供了 WASM-4 运行时，使现代 WASM 游戏能够在功能手机上运行，展示了紧凑运行时如何让老设备焕发新生，并接入现代网页式体验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>W4ME Station ships a WASM-4 runtime compatible with Java ME devices, letting modern WASM-based games run on feature phones. It demonstrates how compact runtimes can bridge old hardware with contemporary web-like experiences.</span><span class='zh'>W4ME Station 为 Java ME 手机提供了 WASM-4 运行时，使现代 WASM 游戏能够在功能手机上运行，展示了紧凑运行时如何让老设备焕发新生，并接入现代网页式体验。</span></p>
